    45	void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    46	    for (auto& body : bodies) {
    47	        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    48	        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    49	        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    50	        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    51	        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    52	        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    53	    }
    54	}
    55	
    56	// Each CUDA thread owns one target body.  Source positions are staged in shared
    57	// memory so that every global load supplies an entire block of target bodies.
    58	// Velocity and position updates deliberately use separate kernels: this keeps
    59	// the force calculation dependent only on positions from the preceding step.
    60	__global__ void computeVelocityKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int numBodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
    61	    __shared__ double sourceX[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
    62	    __shared__ double sourceY[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
    63	    __shared__ double sourceZ[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
    64	
    65	    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
    66	    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + lane;
    67	    const bool active = localIndex < localCount;
    68	    const int bodyIndex = firstBody + localIndex;
    69	
    70	    const double x = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.x : 0.0;
    71	    const double y = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.y : 0.0;
    72	    const double z = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.z : 0.0;
    73	    double forceX = 0.0;
    74	    double forceY = 0.0;
    75	    double forceZ = 0.0;
    76	
    77	    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE) {
    78	        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
    79	        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
    80	            sourceX[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.x;
    81	            sourceY[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.y;
    82	            sourceZ[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.z;
    83	        }
    84	        __syncthreads();
    85	
    86	        const int tileSize = min(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
    87	        if (active) {
    88	            for (int source = 0; source < tileSize; ++source) {
    89	                const double dx = sourceX[source] - x;
    90	                const double dy = sourceY[source] - y;
    91	                const double dz = sourceZ[source] - z;
    92	                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
    93	                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
    94	                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
    95	
    96	                forceX += dx * invDist3;
    97	                forceY += dy * invDist3;
    98	                forceZ += dz * invDist3;
    99	            }
   100	        }
   101	        __syncthreads();
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    if (active) {
   105	        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.x += DT * forceX;
   106	        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.y += DT * forceY;
   107	        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
   108	    }
   109	}
   110	
   111	__global__ void integrateKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
   112	    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   113	    if (localIndex >= localCount) {
   114	        return;
   115	    }
   116	
   117	    Body& body = bodies[firstBody + localIndex];
   118	    body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
   119	    body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
   120	    body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
   121	}
   122	
   123	// This CPU portion runs concurrently with the GPU portion.  It is useful on
   124	// GPU clusters with substantial host CPU capacity and is load-balanced from
   125	// measured rates after each step.
   126	void computeVelocitiesCpu(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int firstBody, int count) {
   127	    if (count == 0) {
   128	        return;
   129	    }
   130	
   131	    const int lastBody = firstBody + count;
   132	    const int threadCount = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(), count);
   133	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(threadCount)
   134	    for (int i = firstBody; i < lastBody; ++i) {
   135	        const double x = bodies[i].pos.x;
   136	        const double y = bodies[i].pos.y;
   137	        const double z = bodies[i].pos.z;
   138	        double forceX = 0.0;
   139	        double forceY = 0.0;
   140	        double forceZ = 0.0;
   141	
   142	        for (int j = 0; j < static_cast<int>(bodies.size()); ++j) {
   143	            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - x;
   144	            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - y;
   145	            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - z;
   146	            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
   147	            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
   148	            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
   149	
   150	            forceX += dx * invDist3;
   151	            forceY += dy * invDist3;
   152	            forceZ += dz * invDist3;
   153	        }
   154	
   155	        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * forceX;
   156	        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * forceY;
   157	        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
   158	    }
   159	}
   160	
   161	void integrateBodiesCpu(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int firstBody, int count) {
   162	    if (count == 0) {
   163	        return;
   164	    }
   165	
   166	    const int lastBody = firstBody + count;
   167	    const int threadCount = std::min(omp_get_max_threads(), count);
   168	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) num_threads(threadCount)
   169	    for (int i = firstBody; i < lastBody; ++i) {
   170	        bodies[i].pos.x += bodies[i].vel.x * DT;
   171	        bodies[i].pos.y += bodies[i].vel.y * DT;
   172	        bodies[i].pos.z += bodies[i].vel.z * DT;
   173	    }
   174	}
   175	
   176	double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   177	    double energy = 0.0;
   178	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
   179	
   180	    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
   181	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   182	        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
   183	    }
   184	
   185	    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
   186	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   187	        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
   188	            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
   189	            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
   190	            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
   191	            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
   192	            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
   193	        }
   194	    }
   195	
   196	    return energy;
   197	}
   198	
   199	bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   200	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   201	        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
   202	            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
   203	            std::printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
   204	            return false;
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        constexpr double maxPos = 1e6;
   208	        constexpr double maxVel = 1e6;
   209	        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
   210	            std::printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   211	            return false;
   212	        }
   213	        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
   214	            std::printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   215	            return false;
   216	        }
   217	    }
   218	    return true;
   219	}
   220	
   221	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   222	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   223	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   224	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
   225	    std::printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
   226	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
   227	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   228	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   229	}
   230	
   231	int initialCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int numBodies) {
   232	    if (localCount < 2 || numBodies == 0 || omp_get_max_threads() < 2) {
   233	        return 0;
   234	    }
   235	
   236	    // A small initial slice is measured and then adjusted.  Avoid scheduling a
   237	    // CPU team for tiny kernels, where launch and OpenMP setup costs dominate.
   238	    const int initialCount = std::max(1, localCount / 64);
   239	    constexpr long long minimumCpuInteractions = 50LL * 1000LL * 1000LL;
   240	    if (static_cast<long long>(initialCount) * numBodies < minimumCpuInteractions) {
   241	        return 0;
   242	    }
   243	    return std::min(initialCount, localCount - 1);
   244	}
   245	
   246	int rebalanceCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int previousCpuCount, float cpuMilliseconds, float gpuMilliseconds) {
   247	    const int gpuCount = localCount - previousCpuCount;
   248	    if (previousCpuCount == 0 || gpuCount == 0 || cpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F || gpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F) {
   249	        return previousCpuCount;
   250	    }
   251	
   252	    const double cpuRate = previousCpuCount / static_cast<double>(cpuMilliseconds);
   253	    const double gpuRate = gpuCount / static_cast<double>(gpuMilliseconds);
   254	    const int balancedCount = static_cast<int>(std::lround(localCount * cpuRate / (cpuRate + gpuRate)));
   255	    return std::clamp(balancedCount, 1, localCount - 1);
   256	}

   258	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   259	    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
   260	    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &providedThreadLevel);
   261	
   262	    int rank = 0;
   263	    int worldSize = 1;
   264	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   265	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   266	    if (providedThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
   267	        if (rank == 0) {
   268	            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI does not provide the MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED thread level required by nbody\n");
   269	        }
   270	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   271	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   272	    }
   273	
   274	    int localRank = 0;
   275	    int localSize = 1;
   276	    MPI_Comm localComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   277	    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &localComm);
   278	    MPI_Comm_rank(localComm, &localRank);
   279	    MPI_Comm_size(localComm, &localSize);
   280	
   281	    // Honour an explicit OpenMP setting; otherwise avoid oversubscribing CPUs
   282	    // when one MPI rank is launched for each GPU on a node.
   283	    if (std::getenv("OMP_NUM_THREADS") == nullptr) {
   284	        omp_set_num_threads(std::max(1, omp_get_num_procs() / localSize));
   285	    }
   286	
   287	    int deviceCount = 0;
   288	    CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
   289	    if (deviceCount == 0) {
   290	        if (rank == 0) {
   291	            std::fprintf(stderr, "nbody requires at least one CUDA device per MPI node\n");
   292	        }
   293	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   294	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   295	    }
   296	    CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));
   297	    MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);
   298	
   299	    int numBodies = 1024;
   300	    int numSteps = 10;
   301	    bool validate = false;
   302	    bool printResults = false;
   303	
   304	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   305	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   306	            numBodies = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   307	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   308	            numSteps = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   309	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   310	            validate = true;
   311	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   312	            printResults = true;
   313	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   314	            if (rank == 0) {
   315	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   316	            }
   317	            MPI_Finalize();
   318	            return EXIT_SUCCESS;
   319	        } else {
   320	            if (rank == 0) {
   321	                std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   322	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   323	            }
   324	            MPI_Finalize();
   325	            return EXIT_FAILURE;
   326	        }
   327	    }
   328	
   329	    if (numBodies < 0 || numSteps < 0) {
   330	        if (rank == 0) {
   331	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Number of bodies and steps must be non-negative\n");
   332	        }
   333	        MPI_Finalize();
   334	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   335	    }
   336	
   337	    if (rank == 0) {
   338	        std::printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
   339	        std::printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
   340	        std::printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
   341	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   342	        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", worldSize, omp_get_max_threads());
   343	    }
   344	
   345	    std::vector<int> receiveCounts(worldSize);
   346	    std::vector<int> displacements(worldSize);
   347	    const int baseCount = numBodies / worldSize;
   348	    const int remainder = numBodies % worldSize;
   349	    int displacement = 0;
   350	    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
   351	        receiveCounts[process] = baseCount + (process < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   352	        displacements[process] = displacement;
   353	        displacement += receiveCounts[process];
   354	    }
   355	    const int localCount = receiveCounts[rank];
   356	    const int firstBody = displacements[rank];
   357	
   358	    MPI_Datatype mpiBody;
   359	    MPI_Type_contiguous(6, MPI_DOUBLE, &mpiBody);
   360	    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBody);
   361	
   362	    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
   363	    if (rank == 0) {
   364	        randomizeBodies(bodies);
   365	    }
   366	    if (numBodies > 0) {
   367	        MPI_Bcast(bodies.data(), numBodies, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   368	    }
   369	
   370	    Body* deviceBodies = nullptr;
   371	    if (numBodies > 0) {
   372	        CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaMalloc(&deviceBodies, static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * sizeof(Body)));
   373	        CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaMemcpy(deviceBodies, bodies.data(), static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * sizeof(Body),
   374	                                    cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   375	    }
   376	
   377	    cudaEvent_t gpuStart;
   378	    cudaEvent_t gpuStop;
   379	    CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaEventCreate(&gpuStart));
   380	    CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaEventCreate(&gpuStop));
   381	
   382	    int cpuCount = initialCpuTargetCount(localCount, numBodies);
   383	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   384	    const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
   385	
   386	    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
   387	        const int gpuCount = localCount - cpuCount;
   388	        CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaEventRecord(gpuStart));
   389	        if (gpuCount > 0) {
   390	            const int blocks = (gpuCount + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE;
   391	            computeVelocityKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(deviceBodies, numBodies, firstBody + cpuCount, gpuCount);
   392	            CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaGetLastError());
   393	            integrateKernel<<<blocks, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(deviceBodies, firstBody + cpuCount, gpuCount);
   394	            CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaGetLastError());
   395	        }
   396	        CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaEventRecord(gpuStop));
   397	
   398	        const auto cpuStart = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
   399	        computeVelocitiesCpu(bodies, firstBody, cpuCount);
   400	        integrateBodiesCpu(bodies, firstBody, cpuCount);
   401	        const auto cpuStop = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
   402	
   403	        CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaEventSynchronize(gpuStop));
   404	        float gpuMilliseconds = 0.0F;
   405	        CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaEventElapsedTime(&gpuMilliseconds, gpuStart, gpuStop));
   406	        const float cpuMilliseconds =
   407	            std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::duration<float, std::milli>>(cpuStop - cpuStart).count();
   408	
   409	        if (gpuCount > 0) {
   410	            CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaMemcpy(bodies.data() + firstBody + cpuCount, deviceBodies + firstBody + cpuCount,
   411	                                        static_cast<size_t>(gpuCount) * sizeof(Body), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   412	        }
   413	
   414	        if (numBodies > 0) {

+// Each CUDA thread owns one target body.  Source positions are staged in shared
+// memory so that every global load supplies an entire block of target bodies.
+// Velocity and position updates deliberately use separate kernels: this keeps
+// the force calculation dependent only on positions from the preceding step.
+__global__ void computeVelocityKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int numBodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    __shared__ double sourceX[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceY[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceZ[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + lane;
+    const bool active = localIndex < localCount;
+    const int bodyIndex = firstBody + localIndex;
+
+    const double x = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.x : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.y : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.z : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            sourceX[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.x;
+            sourceY[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.y;
+            sourceZ[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.z;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int tileSize = min(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+        if (active) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < tileSize; ++source) {
+                const double dx = sourceX[source] - x;
+                const double dy = sourceY[source] - y;
+                const double dz = sourceZ[source] - z;
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
     }
 }

+int initialCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int numBodies) {
+    if (localCount < 2 || numBodies == 0 || omp_get_max_threads() < 2) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    // A small initial slice is measured and then adjusted.  Avoid scheduling a
+    // CPU team for tiny kernels, where launch and OpenMP setup costs dominate.
+    const int initialCount = std::max(1, localCount / 64);
+    constexpr long long minimumCpuInteractions = 50LL * 1000LL * 1000LL;
+    if (static_cast<long long>(initialCount) * numBodies < minimumCpuInteractions) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    return std::min(initialCount, localCount - 1);
+}
+
+int rebalanceCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int previousCpuCount, float cpuMilliseconds, float gpuMilliseconds) {
+    const int gpuCount = localCount - previousCpuCount;
+    if (previousCpuCount == 0 || gpuCount == 0 || cpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F || gpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F) {
+        return previousCpuCount;
+    }
+
+    const double cpuRate = previousCpuCount / static_cast<double>(cpuMilliseconds);
+    const double gpuRate = gpuCount / static_cast<double>(gpuMilliseconds);
+    const int balancedCount = static_cast<int>(std::lround(localCount * cpuRate / (cpuRate + gpuRate)));
+    return std::clamp(balancedCount, 1, localCount - 1);
+}
+

codex
I found one useful scalability refinement during review: forces only depend on positions, so the per-step MPI synchronization can exchange three doubles per body rather than the entire six-double state. I’m applying that reduction while retaining a final full-state gather only when results or validation need it.
apply patch

+// Each CUDA thread owns one target body.  Source positions are staged in shared
+// memory so that every global load supplies an entire block of target bodies.
+// Velocity and position updates deliberately use separate kernels: this keeps
+// the force calculation dependent only on positions from the preceding step.
+__global__ void computeVelocityKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int numBodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    __shared__ double sourceX[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceY[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceZ[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + lane;
+    const bool active = localIndex < localCount;
+    const int bodyIndex = firstBody + localIndex;
+
+    const double x = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.x : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.y : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.z : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            sourceX[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.x;
+            sourceY[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.y;
+            sourceZ[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.z;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int tileSize = min(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+        if (active) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < tileSize; ++source) {
+                const double dx = sourceX[source] - x;
+                const double dy = sourceY[source] - y;
+                const double dz = sourceZ[source] - z;
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void integrateKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localIndex >= localCount) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    Body& body = bodies[firstBody + localIndex];
+    body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+    body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+    body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
 }

+int initialCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int numBodies) {
+    if (localCount < 2 || numBodies == 0 || omp_get_max_threads() < 2) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    // A small initial slice is measured and then adjusted.  Avoid scheduling a
+    // CPU team for tiny kernels, where launch and OpenMP setup costs dominate.
+    const int initialCount = std::max(1, localCount / 64);
+    constexpr long long minimumCpuInteractions = 50LL * 1000LL * 1000LL;
+    if (static_cast<long long>(initialCount) * numBodies < minimumCpuInteractions) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    return std::min(initialCount, localCount - 1);
+}
+
+int rebalanceCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int previousCpuCount, float cpuMilliseconds, float gpuMilliseconds) {
+    const int gpuCount = localCount - previousCpuCount;
+    if (previousCpuCount == 0 || gpuCount == 0 || cpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F || gpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F) {
+        return previousCpuCount;
+    }
+
+    const double cpuRate = previousCpuCount / static_cast<double>(cpuMilliseconds);
+    const double gpuRate = gpuCount / static_cast<double>(gpuMilliseconds);
+    const int balancedCount = static_cast<int>(std::lround(localCount * cpuRate / (cpuRate + gpuRate)));
+    return std::clamp(balancedCount, 1, localCount - 1);
 }
 
 int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
+    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &providedThreadLevel);
+
+    int rank = 0;
+    int worldSize = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
+    if (providedThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI does not provide the MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED thread level required by nbody\n");
+        }

+

+        return EXIT_FAILURE;

+    CUDA_CHECK(rank, cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));
+    MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);

+}
+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one target body.  Source positions are staged in shared
+// memory so that every global load supplies an entire block of target bodies.
+// Velocity and position updates deliberately use separate kernels: this keeps
+// the force calculation dependent only on positions from the preceding step.
+__global__ void computeVelocityKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int numBodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    __shared__ double sourceX[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceY[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceZ[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + lane;
+    const bool active = localIndex < localCount;
+    const int bodyIndex = firstBody + localIndex;
+
+    const double x = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.x : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.y : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.z : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            sourceX[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.x;
+            sourceY[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.y;
+            sourceZ[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.z;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int tileSize = min(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+        if (active) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < tileSize; ++source) {
+                const double dx = sourceX[source] - x;
+                const double dy = sourceY[source] - y;
+                const double dz = sourceZ[source] - z;
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void integrateKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localIndex >= localCount) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    Body& body = bodies[firstBody + localIndex];
+    body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+    body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+    body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
 }

+        double forceX = 0.0;

+            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - x;
+            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - y;
+            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - z;
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;

-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;

+    std::printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+}
+
+int initialCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int numBodies) {
+    if (localCount < 2 || numBodies == 0 || omp_get_max_threads() < 2) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    // A small initial slice is measured and then adjusted.  Avoid scheduling a
+    // CPU team for tiny kernels, where launch and OpenMP setup costs dominate.
+    const int initialCount = std::max(1, localCount / 64);
+    constexpr long long minimumCpuInteractions = 50LL * 1000LL * 1000LL;
+    if (static_cast<long long>(initialCount) * numBodies < minimumCpuInteractions) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    return std::min(initialCount, localCount - 1);
+}
+
+int rebalanceCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int previousCpuCount, float cpuMilliseconds, float gpuMilliseconds) {
+    const int gpuCount = localCount - previousCpuCount;
+    if (previousCpuCount == 0 || gpuCount == 0 || cpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F || gpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F) {
+        return previousCpuCount;
+    }
+
+    const double cpuRate = previousCpuCount / static_cast<double>(cpuMilliseconds);
+    const double gpuRate = gpuCount / static_cast<double>(gpuMilliseconds);
+    const int balancedCount = static_cast<int>(std::lround(localCount * cpuRate / (cpuRate + gpuRate)));
+    return std::clamp(balancedCount, 1, localCount - 1);
 }

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithCudaError(cudaError_t error, const char* expression, int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));

+}
+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one target body.  Source positions are staged in shared
+// memory so that every global load supplies an entire block of target bodies.
+// Velocity and position updates deliberately use separate kernels: this keeps
+// the force calculation dependent only on positions from the preceding step.
+__global__ void computeVelocityKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int numBodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    __shared__ double sourceX[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceY[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceZ[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + lane;
+    const bool active = localIndex < localCount;
+    const int bodyIndex = firstBody + localIndex;
+
+    const double x = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.x : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.y : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.z : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            sourceX[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.x;
+            sourceY[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.y;
+            sourceZ[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.z;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int tileSize = min(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+        if (active) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < tileSize; ++source) {
+                const double dx = sourceX[source] - x;
+                const double dy = sourceY[source] - y;
+                const double dz = sourceZ[source] - z;
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void integrateKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localIndex >= localCount) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    Body& body = bodies[firstBody + localIndex];
+    body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+    body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+    body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
 }

-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;

+// GPU clusters with substantial host CPU capacity and is load-balanced from
+// measured rates after each step.
+void computeVelocitiesCpu(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int firstBody, int count) {
+    if (count == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const int lastBody = firstBody + count;

+        double forceX = 0.0;
+        double forceY = 0.0;
+        double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+        for (int j = 0; j < static_cast<int>(bodies.size()); ++j) {
+            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - x;
+            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - y;
+            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - z;
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+
+            forceX += dx * invDist3;
+            forceY += dy * invDist3;
+            forceZ += dz * invDist3;
         }

+
+int initialCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int numBodies) {
+    if (localCount < 2 || numBodies == 0 || omp_get_max_threads() < 2) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    // A small initial slice is measured and then adjusted.  Avoid scheduling a
+    // CPU team for tiny kernels, where launch and OpenMP setup costs dominate.
+    const int initialCount = std::max(1, localCount / 64);
+    constexpr long long minimumCpuInteractions = 50LL * 1000LL * 1000LL;
+    if (static_cast<long long>(initialCount) * numBodies < minimumCpuInteractions) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    return std::min(initialCount, localCount - 1);
+}
+
+int rebalanceCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int previousCpuCount, float cpuMilliseconds, float gpuMilliseconds) {
+    const int gpuCount = localCount - previousCpuCount;
+    if (previousCpuCount == 0 || gpuCount == 0 || cpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F || gpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F) {
+        return previousCpuCount;
+    }
+
+    const double cpuRate = previousCpuCount / static_cast<double>(cpuMilliseconds);
+    const double gpuRate = gpuCount / static_cast<double>(gpuMilliseconds);
+    const int balancedCount = static_cast<int>(std::lround(localCount * cpuRate / (cpuRate + gpuRate)));
+    return std::clamp(balancedCount, 1, localCount - 1);
 }

+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));

+        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
+    }
+}
+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one target body.  Source positions are staged in shared
+// memory so that every global load supplies an entire block of target bodies.
+// Velocity and position updates deliberately use separate kernels: this keeps
+// the force calculation dependent only on positions from the preceding step.
+__global__ void computeVelocityKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int numBodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    __shared__ double sourceX[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceY[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceZ[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + lane;
+    const bool active = localIndex < localCount;
+    const int bodyIndex = firstBody + localIndex;
+
+    const double x = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.x : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.y : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.z : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            sourceX[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.x;
+            sourceY[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.y;
+            sourceZ[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.z;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int tileSize = min(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+        if (active) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < tileSize; ++source) {
+                const double dx = sourceX[source] - x;
+                const double dy = sourceY[source] - y;
+                const double dz = sourceZ[source] - z;
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void integrateKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localIndex >= localCount) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    Body& body = bodies[firstBody + localIndex];
+    body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+    body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+    body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
 }

+int initialCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int numBodies) {
+    if (localCount < 2 || numBodies == 0 || omp_get_max_threads() < 2) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    // A small initial slice is measured and then adjusted.  Avoid scheduling a
+    // CPU team for tiny kernels, where launch and OpenMP setup costs dominate.
+    const int initialCount = std::max(1, localCount / 64);
+    constexpr long long minimumCpuInteractions = 50LL * 1000LL * 1000LL;
+    if (static_cast<long long>(initialCount) * numBodies < minimumCpuInteractions) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    return std::min(initialCount, localCount - 1);
+}
+
+int rebalanceCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int previousCpuCount, float cpuMilliseconds, float gpuMilliseconds) {
+    const int gpuCount = localCount - previousCpuCount;
+    if (previousCpuCount == 0 || gpuCount == 0 || cpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F || gpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F) {
+        return previousCpuCount;
+    }
+
+    const double cpuRate = previousCpuCount / static_cast<double>(cpuMilliseconds);
+    const double gpuRate = gpuCount / static_cast<double>(gpuMilliseconds);
+    const int balancedCount = static_cast<int>(std::lround(localCount * cpuRate / (cpuRate + gpuRate)));
+    return std::clamp(balancedCount, 1, localCount - 1);
 }

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithCudaError(cudaError_t error, const char* expression, int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));

+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one target body.  Source positions are staged in shared
+// memory so that every global load supplies an entire block of target bodies.
+// Velocity and position updates deliberately use separate kernels: this keeps
+// the force calculation dependent only on positions from the preceding step.
+__global__ void computeVelocityKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int numBodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    __shared__ double sourceX[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceY[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceZ[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + lane;
+    const bool active = localIndex < localCount;
+    const int bodyIndex = firstBody + localIndex;
+
+    const double x = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.x : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.y : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.z : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            sourceX[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.x;
+            sourceY[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.y;
+            sourceZ[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.z;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int tileSize = min(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+        if (active) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < tileSize; ++source) {
+                const double dx = sourceX[source] - x;
+                const double dy = sourceY[source] - y;
+                const double dz = sourceZ[source] - z;
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void integrateKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localIndex >= localCount) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    Body& body = bodies[firstBody + localIndex];
+    body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+    body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+    body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
 }

+int initialCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int numBodies) {
+    if (localCount < 2 || numBodies == 0 || omp_get_max_threads() < 2) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    // A small initial slice is measured and then adjusted.  Avoid scheduling a
+    // CPU team for tiny kernels, where launch and OpenMP setup costs dominate.
+    const int initialCount = std::max(1, localCount / 64);
+    constexpr long long minimumCpuInteractions = 50LL * 1000LL * 1000LL;
+    if (static_cast<long long>(initialCount) * numBodies < minimumCpuInteractions) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    return std::min(initialCount, localCount - 1);
+}
+
+int rebalanceCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int previousCpuCount, float cpuMilliseconds, float gpuMilliseconds) {
+    const int gpuCount = localCount - previousCpuCount;
+    if (previousCpuCount == 0 || gpuCount == 0 || cpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F || gpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F) {
+        return previousCpuCount;
+    }
+
+    const double cpuRate = previousCpuCount / static_cast<double>(cpuMilliseconds);
+    const double gpuRate = gpuCount / static_cast<double>(gpuMilliseconds);
+    const int balancedCount = static_cast<int>(std::lround(localCount * cpuRate / (cpuRate + gpuRate)));
+    return std::clamp(balancedCount, 1, localCount - 1);
 }

+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one target body.  Source positions are staged in shared
+// memory so that every global load supplies an entire block of target bodies.
+// Velocity and position updates deliberately use separate kernels: this keeps
+// the force calculation dependent only on positions from the preceding step.
+__global__ void computeVelocityKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int numBodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    __shared__ double sourceX[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceY[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceZ[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + lane;
+    const bool active = localIndex < localCount;
+    const int bodyIndex = firstBody + localIndex;
+
+    const double x = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.x : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.y : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.z : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            sourceX[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.x;
+            sourceY[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.y;
+            sourceZ[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.z;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int tileSize = min(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+        if (active) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < tileSize; ++source) {
+                const double dx = sourceX[source] - x;
+                const double dy = sourceY[source] - y;
+                const double dz = sourceZ[source] - z;
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void integrateKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localIndex >= localCount) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    Body& body = bodies[firstBody + localIndex];
+    body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+    body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+    body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
 }

+int initialCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int numBodies) {
+    if (localCount < 2 || numBodies == 0 || omp_get_max_threads() < 2) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    // A small initial slice is measured and then adjusted.  Avoid scheduling a
+    // CPU team for tiny kernels, where launch and OpenMP setup costs dominate.
+    const int initialCount = std::max(1, localCount / 64);
+    constexpr long long minimumCpuInteractions = 50LL * 1000LL * 1000LL;
+    if (static_cast<long long>(initialCount) * numBodies < minimumCpuInteractions) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    return std::min(initialCount, localCount - 1);
+}
+
+int rebalanceCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int previousCpuCount, float cpuMilliseconds, float gpuMilliseconds) {
+    const int gpuCount = localCount - previousCpuCount;
+    if (previousCpuCount == 0 || gpuCount == 0 || cpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F || gpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F) {
+        return previousCpuCount;
+    }
+
+    const double cpuRate = previousCpuCount / static_cast<double>(cpuMilliseconds);
+    const double gpuRate = gpuCount / static_cast<double>(gpuMilliseconds);
+    const int balancedCount = static_cast<int>(std::lround(localCount * cpuRate / (cpuRate + gpuRate)));
+    return std::clamp(balancedCount, 1, localCount - 1);
 }

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithCudaError(cudaError_t error, const char* expression, int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA error in %s: %s\n", rank, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));

+}
+
+// Each CUDA thread owns one target body.  Source positions are staged in shared
+// memory so that every global load supplies an entire block of target bodies.
+// Velocity and position updates deliberately use separate kernels: this keeps
+// the force calculation dependent only on positions from the preceding step.
+__global__ void computeVelocityKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int numBodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    __shared__ double sourceX[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceY[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ double sourceZ[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x;
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + lane;
+    const bool active = localIndex < localCount;
+    const int bodyIndex = firstBody + localIndex;
+
+    const double x = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.x : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.y : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? bodies[bodyIndex].pos.z : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < numBodies; tileStart += CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE) {
+        const int sourceIndex = tileStart + lane;
+        if (sourceIndex < numBodies) {
+            sourceX[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.x;
+            sourceY[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.y;
+            sourceZ[lane] = bodies[sourceIndex].pos.z;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int tileSize = min(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE, numBodies - tileStart);
+        if (active) {
+            for (int source = 0; source < tileSize; ++source) {
+                const double dx = sourceX[source] - x;
+                const double dy = sourceY[source] - y;
+                const double dz = sourceZ[source] - z;
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                forceX += dx * invDist3;
+                forceY += dy * invDist3;
+                forceZ += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (active) {
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.x += DT * forceX;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.y += DT * forceY;
+        bodies[bodyIndex].vel.z += DT * forceZ;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void integrateKernel(Body* __restrict__ bodies, int firstBody, int localCount) {
+    const int localIndex = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (localIndex >= localCount) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    Body& body = bodies[firstBody + localIndex];
+    body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+    body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+    body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
 }

+void computeVelocitiesCpu(std::vector<Body>& bodies, int firstBody, int count) {

+

+            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - x;

+            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - z;

-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;

 }

+int initialCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int numBodies) {
+    if (localCount < 2 || numBodies == 0 || omp_get_max_threads() < 2) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    // A small initial slice is measured and then adjusted.  Avoid scheduling a
+    // CPU team for tiny kernels, where launch and OpenMP setup costs dominate.
+    const int initialCount = std::max(1, localCount / 64);
+    constexpr long long minimumCpuInteractions = 50LL * 1000LL * 1000LL;
+    if (static_cast<long long>(initialCount) * numBodies < minimumCpuInteractions) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    return std::min(initialCount, localCount - 1);
+}
+
+int rebalanceCpuTargetCount(int localCount, int previousCpuCount, float cpuMilliseconds, float gpuMilliseconds) {
+    const int gpuCount = localCount - previousCpuCount;
+    if (previousCpuCount == 0 || gpuCount == 0 || cpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F || gpuMilliseconds <= 0.0F) {
+        return previousCpuCount;
+    }
+
+    const double cpuRate = previousCpuCount / static_cast<double>(cpuMilliseconds);
+    const double gpuRate = gpuCount / static_cast<double>(gpuMilliseconds);
+    const int balancedCount = static_cast<int>(std::lround(localCount * cpuRate / (cpuRate + gpuRate)));
+    return std::clamp(balancedCount, 1, localCount - 1);
